**The gamble is accepted on the following basis, stated so it can be checked against later rather than re-litigated:** further design-time analysis has run out of leverage --- the one available piece, "this is the same family as spreadsheets and circuit simulation," is real supporting evidence that a scalable version is *possible in principle* (those systems scale via exactly iteration 2/3's bounded-batch-then-prioritized-recompute shape) but cannot say whether *our* graph, with C6's diamonds and C12's directed causal edges layered over an LLM-authored consequence shape, behaves like theirs. Only running iteration 1 answers that. **Iteration 1 must therefore be built to measure, not only to work:** it should report cascade fan-out, diamond frequency, and settle time under naive scheduling as a first-class output, not an afterthought, since that data is what iterations 2 and 3 are designed from and the only way to obtain it short of building them blind. **A fourth measurement added 2026-08-09: fault propagation** --- see [the feedback regime](AGENT.abstractionLayers.planning.md#the-feedback-regime-is-the-thing-to-measure-and-it-decides-which-solutions-to-look-for-2026-08-09). Proxies rather than direct measurement: how often a later reasoning step contradicts an earlier established fact, how deep summarization chains actually run, and whether contradiction frequency **rises with world age**. **That last one is the damping-versus-amplifying question stated as something countable**, and iteration 1 is the first place it can be asked of real data rather than argued.

**Named rollback trigger, set now while it costs nothing to be honest about:** if iteration 1's measured fan-out does not visibly compress under a sketched heat-driven redesign --- i.e., prioritized recompute would still have to touch most of what naive scheduling touches --- P4 as a whole is the thing that failed, not iteration 3's design. **AB-39, AB-40(b), and AB-37 all revert to Open if that happens**, exactly as `P4-i1-dependent` tagging exists to make cheap to discover. **P4 also has known, named costs independent of this trigger** (diamond glitches on DAG membership, cross-noted at H3 clause 6, and oscillation on causal cycles) that iteration 1's instrumentation should surface evidence on, not merely inherit as accepted risk by default.

### The problem: both obvious answers are unbounded, and one of them is impossible

| Path | Cost | Verdict |
| --- | --- | --- |
| **Store all cascading updates immediately on change** | Unbounded write fan-out | **Not merely expensive --- structurally impossible on the shipped kernel.** `computeStepSequenceFootprint` runs **once**, up front, and `MultiKeyUpdate` cannot be re-entered, so a transaction must declare every host it touches *before* walking. **A cascade whose extent is discovered by walking cannot declare its footprint** |
| **Re-derive from first sources on each read** | Unbounded fetch --- and after [C11](AGENT.abstractionLayers.corpus.planning.md#c11-the-rube-goldberg-machine), unbounded in *depth* as well, since no level is privileged | Workable, and pays on the player-facing path |

**That asymmetry is what makes the third path structural rather than preferential.** It is not the compromise between two workable options; it is the only shape that fits a kernel with a once-only footprint.

### The scheme

**Impulses of causal change are persisted into the graph as data.** Repeated waves of processing continue a chain of consequence that earlier passes carried only partway. **The consistency guarantee is given neither at write time nor lazily at read time, but *eventually*, when the graph's energy settles.**

**What it actually buys, stated so nobody defends it as an efficiency:** total work does not go down --- waves re-process, so it likely goes up. What changes is **which cost is bounded**. One unbounded transaction becomes many bounded ones, each able to declare a footprint it can know. **Unbounded *synchronous* becomes bounded synchronous plus unbounded *asynchronous*** --- the right trade precisely because the synchronous path is the player-facing one.

### The substrate is half-built, and the missing half is exactly this half

Verified against [`thinking/AGENT.md`](../../../../../lambda/ephemera/dataSource/thinking/AGENT.md) when this plan mined it on 2026-08-05, before any of this was in view:

- **Shipped:** durable `Meta::Schedule` rows on `TASK#${workItemId}`, the status vocabulary `scheduled | claimed | cancelled | completed`, and a retention policy that explicitly does **not** TTL in-flight rows, so *"opportunistically queued work is not TTL'd while waiting."*
- **Not shipped:** **nothing writes `claimed`.** No worker, no deferred dispatch, no queue anything pulls from.

**A persisted impulse is a durable worklist entry, and a durable worklist is what those rows are.** `Meta::Result`'s `nonCollidingPutItem` --- first write wins, duplicate finalize returns `alreadyFinalized` --- supplies the idempotence a wave system needs when a wave is processed twice.

**Note the pattern rather than the instance.** This is the sixth time this initiative has found infrastructure pre-registered for a need it had not yet articulated. **The rule that follows: look for the pre-registration before designing anything new here.**

### Retraction dissolves under a dataflow reading, and that reading is the ludic-native one

**The problem as first posed.** Turning power *on* propagates naturally; turning it *off* after a cut is a **retraction**, and a naive relaxation never un-powers the door because nothing gives the door a reason to re-examine itself. That is the classic truth-maintenance problem, and it is genuinely harder than the direction the scheme handles well.

**The reframe, and it is a different computational model rather than a restatement:** *turning off the power is not the retraction of a positive state --- it is its own change, propagating as any other change does.*

| | Truth-maintenance | **Dataflow** |
| --- | --- | --- |
| What a node holds | Facts, with justifications | **A current value** |
| Removing support | Find dependents, unwind --- absence has no representation to propagate | **Does not arise.** A new value arrives and flows |
| What justifies a value | A tracked justification structure | **The graph itself** --- every dependent is visited by construction |

**Under dataflow there is no retraction, only a new value.** Spreadsheets, reactive systems and circuit simulation are the right family. **The graph holds *states*, not *beliefs*** --- and "retraction" was an artifact of modelling the world as a knowledge base rather than as a world. **Same instinct that produced *ludic* as a name, arriving at the algorithm instead of the vocabulary.**

**Where the cost went, because it did not vanish.** Dataflow is **eager** where truth-maintenance can be lazy: cutting the trunk visits every downstream device, including the ones nobody will look at. Total work becomes proportional to the **graph**, not to what anyone asked. **Here that is the right trade rather than merely a different one** --- eager full fan-out is exactly what waves are good at, since each frontier is bounded and declarable. It is also what [narrative heat](#narrative-heat-the-optimization-that-is-probably-not-only-an-optimization) exists to prune.

#### The one wrinkle that survives: topology change

**Value changes propagate. Topology changes do not, because the thing that would carry the impulse is the thing being removed.**

The generator going dark is a value change on a node --- push it downstream. **Cutting the cable is an edge deletion**, and in a push model there is no longer an edge to push along. Nothing tells the door it lost an input.

**The rule that follows: edge deletion emits its own impulse before it ceases to exist.** The cut knows both endpoints; it delivers *you lost an input* to the downstream one as part of the cut, then removes the edge.

**And the codebase already forbids silent edge disappearance.** `removeObject` **asserts and throws** when a relational edge still references a node, deliberately with no silent-strip variant (BD-33/35), and [`boundaryEdgeOutcomes`](../../../../../lambda/ephemera/dataSource/positions/ludicGraph/expandValidate/interactionUnderTransfer.ts) classifies every severed edge **carry / dissolve / defer / error**. **Fifth arrival of the boundary-edge discipline in a case outside its brief** --- and the least expected, since it was written for carrying a chest through a doorway.

#### Two defects inherited with the model

1. **Glitches on diamonds.** A node with two inputs tracing to a common ancestor can be reached by the short path before the long one and briefly compute a value that was never true. **[AB-13](AGENT.abstractionLayers.corpus.planning.md#c6-the-rope-the-candle-and-the-impromptu-timer)'s DAG makes this real rather than theoretical** --- one part, two wholes, is the diamond. Fixes: topological ordering of the worklist, or accepting transient glitches.
2. **Oscillation on cycles.** Generator powers pump, pump cools generator, and that is ordinary fiction. **Composition is a DAG; the causal kind is not**, so termination needs a guard rather than a structural guarantee --- `computeCarryClosure`'s `closureSet.has` is the shipped precedent. The dataflow framing does not fix cycles but it makes the failure **legible**: oscillation you can detect rather than regress you cannot.

### Narrative heat: the optimization that is probably not only an optimization

**The waste, made concrete.** [Ariadne's thread](AGENT.abstractionLayers.corpus.planning.md#c7-ariadnes-thread) strung through thirty labyrinth rooms. A naive cascade --- impulses along every link, no decay, no differentiation --- eventually proposes *"the string goes briefly taut"* to all thirty. **In actual play most of those rooms are empty**, and that calculation is the noise of a tree falling in an unoccupied forest.

**The scheme, in two halves.**

1. **The network cascades a second quantity besides causality: *narrative heat*.** Places people are generate more heat than empty rooms; things people are interacting with are hotter than things they ignore. **Heat cascades as a heat map in the *reverse* direction of links.**
2. **Change-impulses carry an energy payload** that **decays over traverse**, **splits on splits**, and **reinforces as it encounters heat** --- so impulses stochastically **travel toward heat**.

**Prior art, named because it tells us where the failure modes are.** Closest is **importance sampling with Russian-roulette termination** from path tracing, which faces this exact shape --- unbounded transport, most contributions negligible --- and has a rigorous answer about when stochastic termination is *safe*: kill paths probabilistically, but scale survivors so the expected result stays exact. **Unbiased, not merely cheap.** Also adjacent: ant-colony pheromone gradients (reverse-propagated attractant plus stochastic traversal, nearly one-to-one) and attention weighting.

#### The distinction the scheme requires: narration versus state

**Two different things travel the same links, and decay is safe for only one of them.**

| Impulse kind | Example | Decay in a cold room |
| --- | --- | --- |
| **Transient / narration** | *"the string goes briefly taut"* | **Free and correct.** Nobody hears it, nothing persists. This is the tree in the empty forest, and the argument for heat is exactly right |
| **Persistent / state** | *"the string is now cut"* | **Lossy.** Segment 27 stays wrong --- and stays wrong **forever**, surfacing an hour later when someone walks in and finds a taut string that should be slack |

**This is a property of the impulse, not of the edge.** The same `part-of` link carries both, so it belongs in its own column rather than folding into [the relation-kind register](AGENT.abstractionLayers.planning.md#the-relation-kind-register).

#### Heat rising must pull unsettled state to settle

**The repair falls out of the same mechanism, which is the main reason to think the scheme is sound rather than merely clever.** A character entering a cold room **is** a heat spike --- and it is also **the read**, the exact moment the state must be correct.

**So heat works in both directions in time:** it guides impulses forward in space, and a heat *increase* reaches back and finishes the ones that stalled. That is lazy evaluation reintroduced precisely at the boundary where laziness is correct, and it is what keeps cold regions from diverging permanently.

**It locks together with the tri-state.** A room whose state is `unsettled` is a room whose pending impulses must resolve before it answers. **Fourth independent arrival at that requirement** --- after coarsening ([C12](AGENT.abstractionLayers.corpus.planning.md#c12-the-moonbase-power-system-and-the-cut-cable)), reads-during-settling, and Coyote observability --- and the first where it is load-bearing rather than tidy.

#### Decay to *parked*, never to *dead* (PROVISIONAL: challenged on two counts)

**Stochastic termination has a failure mode that reads terribly in a game:** *the trap door did not fire because we rolled badly.* Non-reproducible, undebuggable, and --- in the prototype setting --- indistinguishable from a dropped wave, which is itself indistinguishable from comic timing.

**So split the stochasticity:** **stochastic in scheduling priority** (which impulses process first --- where the optimization actually lives), **deterministic in termination** (an impulse below threshold is **parked**, never discarded). A parked impulse is a durable low-priority worklist entry that a heat rise promotes.

**And the shipped retention policy already describes exactly this:** in-flight `Meta::Schedule` rows *"do not set `deleteAt`, so opportunistically queued work is not TTL'd while waiting."* **A parked impulse waiting for heat is opportunistically queued work.**

**Recorded as provisional 2026-08-07.** Two objections, and they are not objections to parking --- they are objections to *never dead*, which is a stronger claim than the argument above earns.

**Objection 1: the parked set is an unbounded history.** Nothing above bounds it. It grows with every below-threshold impulse the world has ever produced, and the cost is not primarily storage --- it is that **a heat rise promotes the whole accumulation at once.** A room that goes cold for a simulated year and then has a character walk into it faces a settle proportional to a year of events, at exactly the moment a read is waiting. The mechanism advertised as the cheap path has its worst case precisely where its correctness guarantee is invoked.

**Objection 2: it aspires to the wrong discipline.** *Never dead* is a promise that **every causal lead is eventually followed up, regardless of whether anyone ever cared.** That is not how fiction works and not how a world should be run. There has to be a way to say **"this happened, it might have mattered if anyone had cared, nobody did, and we are not going to keep pushing it"** --- and the scheme as written has no such statement, only indefinite deferral.

**What the original argument actually supported.** The trap-door complaint is about **silent, non-reproducible** loss. It is satisfied by a *recorded* termination --- *this impulse decayed below threshold, here, then* --- not by keeping the impulse **live**. Those are different properties, and the section conflated them:

| Property | What it buys | Was it argued for? |
| --- | --- | --- |
| **Auditable** --- termination is recorded | Kills the undebuggable-roll failure mode | **Yes.** This is the whole of the trap-door argument |
| **Revivable** --- a heat rise can still promote it | Cold regions converge on read | **No.** Asserted alongside, never separately justified --- and it is the one the objections hit |

**Three repair directions, none adopted.** Recorded so the next pass argues rather than re-derives:

1. **Collapse rather than queue.** [The dataflow reframe](#retraction-dissolves-under-a-dataflow-reading-and-that-reading-is-the-ludic-native-one) already says *the graph holds states, not beliefs* --- so parked work should be a **pending delta per node**, not a log of impulses. Fifty queued changes to segment 27 collapse to *what segment 27 should now be*. That bounds the parked set by **graph size × relation kind**, not by event count, and it kills objection 1 outright. **This is a consequence of the reframe that was already recorded and not drawn** --- which is reason to suspect it is right, and reason to check it is not merely tidy.
2. **Park only what is not rederivable.** Under dataflow most state is recomputable from current sources, and rederivation from a cold region is affordable *because it is rare and local* --- the trilemma's second path, readmitted at the one scale where it was never the expensive option. If a node can be rederived on heat rise, its parked impulses are redundant and may be discarded. **Parking is then mandatory only for facts with no surviving cause record** --- *the cable was cut* --- which is a much smaller set.
3. **Let absence of heat be terminal, not merely deferring.** The mechanism objection 2 asks for **already exists in the scheme** --- it is heat's absence. What is missing is *permission for it to end something*. The candidate rule: an impulse parked below threshold past some horizon **expires**, auditably, and the node it targeted is marked rederive-on-read rather than settle-on-read.

**And the narration/state split above probably already decides most of it.** Dropping a **transient/narration** impulse nobody was present for is not a defect --- it is correct, and permanently correct; the loss the table describes is entirely in the **persistent/state** column. If so, *never dead* was over-generalised from the one row that needed it. **Not asserted --- it is [PS-11](#open-questions-p4-sub-questions-ids-stable-never-reused).**

#### Split versus replicate is a per-relation-kind fact

**Energy dividing at a branch is conservation --- right for a *pull*, where force genuinely divides between paths. Wrong for a power cut**, which is a broadcast: all thirty downstream devices go dark and none goes one-thirtieth dark. Attenuating across a fan-out would leave a large power system unable to notify its own edges.

**So split-versus-replicate tracks whether the underlying fiction conserves something**, and it is a register entry. **It is also the register's first *prediction* rather than a recorded observation**, which is what that section was opened hoping it would do.

#### Heat's own cost, and a tension to resolve before building it

Heat is itself a reverse cascade from every occupied room, and its source term is **character navigation --- the highest-frequency mutation in the system** (BD-36; it routes through the same `commitStepSequence` as take/drop). On the face of it, one unbounded cascade has become two.

**What saves it: heat has no correctness obligation.** A heat map stale by seconds is fine; a causal state stale by seconds is a bug. So heat may be batched, timer-decayed, approximated, and shed under load --- none of which causality may do. **The asymmetry is what makes the second cascade affordable, and it should be stated as a rule before someone tries to make heat exact.**

**But heat is probably not only an optimization**, and that is where the tension is. *What does anyone care about right now* is the signal at least four things have been reaching for: [C3](AGENT.abstractionLayers.corpus.planning.md#c3-the-snare-trap-coyote-cannot-name)'s Coyote context, which stages a whole room relevant or not and has no relevance filter; **AB-22**'s affinity filter; **AB-24**'s ambient context tier; and the [reasoning axis](AGENT.abstractionLayers.planning.md#the-five-axes-what-the-graphs-flexibility-is-in-service-to) generally, since what belongs in an LLM packet is the hot material. Apprehension wants it too --- what gets described in detail is the hot material.

**Those two claims are in direct tension.** A mechanism several correctness-adjacent consumers depend on cannot be as sloppy as the paragraph above says it is allowed to be. **Resolve that before heat is built, not after** --- most likely by separating a cheap approximate heat used for scheduling from whatever the context consumers actually need, and confirming they are not the same artifact.

### Why CoyoteGame is the prototype, and the seam in that choice

**The artifact this architecture produces is a delay:** a trap door opens, nothing happens for several seconds, and *then* the person standing on it falls. **In most settings that is a defect. In cartoon slapstick it is the genre's defining comic beat**, so the prototype domain was chosen partly because the architecture's characteristic failure is a genre-confirming win there.

**A second, independent reason points at the same prototype**, and it is the stronger one for the record: **[C3](AGENT.abstractionLayers.corpus.planning.md#c3-the-snare-trap-coyote-cannot-name) establishes Coyote is also the domain that most needs the structure** --- `CoyoteStagedObject` carries no edges at all, so a rope tied to a branch and the same objects in a heap produce **byte-identical** prompt context.

**Three seams in the choice, recorded because a comfortable prototype is where measurements do not get taken.**

1. **The genre that forgives the delay never measures it.** Nothing in Coyote will complain that settling took eight seconds rather than eight hundred milliseconds. **Instrument settle time from the first wave anyway** --- Coyote is the only place to measure it under real load without the pressure to fix it first.
2. **In Coyote the delay is load-bearing, so Coyote will push to *preserve* it** --- and then to make it *reliable*, because a gag needs the beat to land consistently rather than varying with worker scheduling. **A reliable beat is a *scheduled* delay, a completely different mechanism from an incidental one.** Keep them separate by construction: incidental latency is an artifact to minimise; a deliberate comic beat is a narration- or trope-level device that asks for itself. Otherwise genre content ends up tuned into the causal substrate --- the instance-taken-for-the-category error already twice in this plan's catalogue.
3. **A dropped impulse and a gag that has not landed yet look identical**, and so does non-termination. **This is the third argument for making settle state explicit** rather than inferring it from *nothing has happened yet*.

**And one consequence for sequencing.** Both motivating cases --- the cut cable and the trap door --- are **removals of support**. That is not coincidence: assertion is fictionally boring and technically easy, while removal is where the drama is and where the algorithm is hard. **Slapstick is almost entirely removal of support**, so the hard direction is what the prototype hits on day one rather than in a later phase. Plan the first slice accordingly.

### What P4 would do to rollback trigger 3

**Moot as originally posed, 2026-08-07: H1 is dead, and not by this trigger firing.** Rollback trigger 3 asked what happens if *the migration cascade proves unplannable* under H1's clause 4/5 whole-migration. [H3](#h3-ports-as-the-boundary-mechanism-decomposition-as-a-modellers-choice) has no whole-migration to be unplannable --- [C9](AGENT.abstractionLayers.corpus.planning.md#c9-coiling-the-rope-back-in) already showed coiling a rope rewrites port egress with no whole ever moving. There is no cascade here for P4 to rescue, because the thing that would have needed rescuing no longer exists.

**Left in place rather than deleted, because its second paragraph turned out to be the more important half and was vindicated independently.** *"Eventual consistency on presence is a different and less obviously acceptable proposition than eventual consistency on state, and it should be argued on its own rather than inherited"* --- written 2026-08-06, before C13 existed. **This is exactly the distinction AB-40 split on 2026-08-07** (see [Open decisions](AGENT.abstractionLayers.planning.md#open-decisions-design--plan-only)), from a completely different direction: taking the fork (presence/position) must stay synchronous, while what `PlaceSetting`'s summary says about it (state/consequence) can settle. Two independent arguments, a day apart, landing on the same line --- worth noting as the kind of convergence this plan has already shown repeatedly (AB-28, AB-29, and C5's guard among them), not as a coincidence to skip past.

### Open questions (P4 sub-questions; ids stable, never reused)

**Parallel to P3's PQ series, and none of these is settled.**

| ID | Question |
| --- | --- |
| **PS-1** | **What is a wave's unit of work** --- one impulse, one frontier, one host, or a time budget? Determines what a wave's footprint declaration looks like |
| **PS-2** | **Who claims?** `Meta::Schedule`'s `claimed` status has no producer. Is the claimer a worker, an opportunistic rider on the next request touching that host, or both? |
| **PS-3** | **What is the settle-state vocabulary**, and is `unsettled` a value in the lattice or a flag beside it? Four consumers now want it; none has specified it |
| **PS-4** | **Is heat one artifact or two?** The scheduling heuristic may be cheap and approximate; the context-relevance signal AB-22/AB-24/C3 want may not be. **Answer before building either** |
| **PS-5** | **Glitch policy** --- topologically ordered worklist, or accept transient wrong values? Coyote will not force the answer, which is why it needs deciding rather than discovering |
| **PS-6** | **Cycle guard** --- iteration cap, damping, or a declared acyclicity constraint per relation kind? |
| **PS-7** | **Does narration ride the same wave as state, or a separate one?** They decay differently (transient vs persistent) and the narration must be delivered in an order a reader can follow, which is [relational narration](../../AGENT.relationalNarration.planning.md)'s standing problem |
| **PS-8** | **What does the unbiasedness result require of us?** Path tracing's Russian roulette is exact in expectation *because survivors are rescaled*. Whether an analogous correction is meaningful for discrete state --- or whether *parked, never dead* makes it moot --- is unexamined. **Amended 2026-08-07:** the *makes it moot* half now leans on a premise that is itself provisional (PS-9), so this row cannot be discharged by appeal to parking |
| **PS-9** | **What bounds the parked set?** Opened 2026-08-07 from the objection that *never dead* is an unbounded history whose worst case lands exactly on a read. **Leading candidate is collapse-not-queue** --- parked work as a **pending delta per node** rather than a log of impulses, bounding it by graph size rather than event count, which [the dataflow reframe](#retraction-dissolves-under-a-dataflow-reading-and-that-reading-is-the-ludic-native-one) arguably already implies. **What must be checked:** whether every relation kind's impulses actually *are* collapsible (order-independent and idempotent on the target), because a kind whose impulses do not commute cannot be collapsed and would keep its queue |
| **PS-10** | **How does a lead die on purpose?** Opened 2026-08-07. The world needs to be able to say *this happened, it might have mattered, nobody cared, stop pushing* --- and the scheme has only indefinite deferral. **Separate the two properties the original argument conflated:** termination must be **auditable** (that is all the trap-door complaint required); it need not be **revivable**. Candidates: expiry past a horizon into an auditable dead record; **rederive-on-read** for any node whose state has surviving sources, so parking is mandatory only for facts with no cause record; or letting sustained absence of heat be terminal rather than merely deferring. **Note this is a fiction requirement before it is a scalability one** --- a world that never drops a thread can never close one |
| **PS-11** | **Is *never dead* only a persistent-state rule?** Opened 2026-08-07. [The narration/state split](#the-distinction-the-scheme-requires-narration-versus-state) says decay is free for transient/narration impulses and lossy only for persistent state. If that is right, *never dead* was over-generalised from the one row that needed it, and PS-9/PS-10 shrink to the state column. **Cheap to settle and it gates the other two, so settle it first** |

### What this does for the name

**A graph is static structure; a network is a medium things propagate through.** If causality works by impulses settling and heat flowing back the other way, ***Ludic Network* stops being a nicer word for the ensemble and starts describing what the ensemble does** --- a second, independent justification for the term recorded under [the naming consequence](AGENT.abstractionLayers.planning.md#the-naming-consequence-positiongraph-will-be-lying), and a stronger one than kind-neutrality alone.

## Proposal P5: improvisational licence as a first-class graph item

**Recorded 2026-08-09 from conversation. Not promoted to a working hypothesis** --- H3 is in flight, and [candidate H2](#candidate-h2-not-adopted-ab-12s-privileged-level-reframe)'s reason for one-at-a-time still holds.

**Status, restated 2026-08-09 after [PL-1's iteration 1](#pl-1-iteration-1-brute-force-relevance-classified-as-a-prototype-2026-08-09) was elected.** P5 splits in two for status purposes, and conflating the halves is what made its promotion question look harder than it is. **The shape half** --- [clauses](#the-scheme-as-a-clause-table-numbered-2026-08-09) 1, 2, 3 and 7 --- **is LOCKED as of 2026-08-09**, after C20 attacked P5 clause 1 and vindicated it while falsifying P5 clause 2 into its repaired content/manner form. See [Locked: P5's shape clauses](#locked-p5s-shape-clauses-2026-08-09) for what is **not** locked and must not ride in on it, and for the proposal-level rollback trigger. **The utility half depends on relevance**, and relevance is now **buildable rather than unsolved**: a candidate is elected, an iteration 2 is named, and rollback triggers are set. **So P5 is blocked on a build, not on an open problem** --- which is the condition under which locking its shape clauses ahead of its utility is honest rather than premature.

**Written as a section rather than a row on this plan's own stated criterion** --- [P2](#proposal-p2-separate-the-storage-shard-from-the-containment-relation) is a section "because it touches AB-2, AB-3, AB-4, AB-9, AB-16, AB-30 and AB-32." P5 touches **AB-9, AB-14, AB-19, AB-29, AB-42, AB-43, AB-44 and AB-45**, reorganizes all four of the negatives-cluster rows, and reaches [C17](AGENT.abstractionLayers.corpus.planning.md#c17-the-professional-kitchen-the-chefs-knife-and-the-stand-mixer-nobody-asked-about) and [C18](AGENT.abstractionLayers.corpus.planning.md#c18-three-place-settings-and-only-two-of-them-may-have-a-napkin). Those rows stay open and are **restated in licence terms** rather than absorbed.

### The scheme, as a clause table (numbered 2026-08-09)

**Improvisational licence is a first-class item in the graph, attached to an entity, and manipulable like anything else in it.**

**Numbered on [H3](#h3-ports-as-the-boundary-mechanism-decomposition-as-a-modellers-choice)'s precedent, and *before* the first falsification case rather than after** --- H3's nine clauses exist so a failure lands somewhere specific and rolls back per clause, and P5 was three clauses of prose carrying a great many consequences. **The exercise paid for itself immediately: numbering surfaced four claims P5 was already relying on without stating** --- P5 clauses 9, 10 and 11, plus a refinement to 7 that its source case had conflated. **An unstated claim cannot be falsified, so those four were unfalsifiable until now**, which is the argument for doing this first.

| # | Clause | Status |
| --- | --- | --- |
| **1** | **Licence is unbounded by default.** An entity with no recorded limit permits any improvisation within its scope | **Established.** [C17](AGENT.abstractionLayers.corpus.planning.md#c17-the-professional-kitchen-the-chefs-knife-and-the-stand-mixer-nobody-asked-about)'s three-value model stated from the other side --- *not established* costs nothing, which is what lets an infinite detail space go unrepresented. **Vindicated 2026-08-09 by [C20](AGENT.abstractionLayers.corpus.planning.md#c20-the-locked-room-and-nothing-obvious), which was built to attack it and found it did not need attacking:** a locked-room mystery needs no closed world, only a high-weight **manner** narrowing (P5 clause 2), so unbounded-by-default survives with the three-value model intact. **Pressure examined and found narrower than first claimed, 2026-08-09.** [Coyote's `environmentAffordances`](#prior-art-in-shipped-code-coyotes-two-affordance-branches-2026-08-09) is a **closed reasoning vocabulary** whose absences are decidable --- but it constrains what a reasoning step is *shown*, not what the world *contains*, and reading it as closed-world was an over-claim withdrawn the same day. **The clause is not falsified.** What it gains is a sharper question for C20: *does a closed reasoning vocabulary license a closed world?* **This is now the clause C20 attacks** --- re-aimed here on 2026-08-09 from P5 clause 3, which turned out to be a wording problem rather than a contradiction. The tightly-authored closed world, where an author wants silence to mean **no**, is the falsification target, and it is **independent of relevance**, which is why it replaced the tavern version |
| **2** | **A negative is a recorded *narrowing* of licence, not a fact asserted about the world.** *There is no deep fryer* is *the kitchen's licence, closed over deep-fryer-shaped things*; a blanket negative is a narrowing to zero. **Narrowings are of two kinds: *content* (a set of things that may not be minted) and *manner* (a way of minting that is not permitted)** | **Established, and falsified 2026-08-09 in an assumption it never stated.** [C20](AGENT.abstractionLayers.corpus.planning.md#c20-the-locked-room-and-nothing-obvious): every worked instance had been **extensional** --- *no deep fryer*, *no napkins*, a place setting closed to further members --- while *no **obvious** entrances* denotes a **predicate that must be evaluated**, undecidable by lookup at any vocabulary size. **The manner/content split above is the repair.** **Two consequences:** a **second judgment point** beside PL-1's (whether a candidate falls *inside* a narrowing, not only which narrowings are relevant), and **AB-43 gets materially worse** --- some regions have no descriptive answer, only a predicate. **Found by numbering the clauses**, which is what that exercise was for: an unstated assumption cannot be falsified. The inversion [C18](AGENT.abstractionLayers.corpus.planning.md#c18-three-place-settings-and-only-two-of-them-may-have-a-napkin) Finding 5 recorded, and what dissolved AB-43's blanket half. Also what keeps C17's closed-world defect from returning: a narrowing is not a completeness claim |
| **3** | **Scope is the entity the licence attaches to, and an entity's scope includes what it contains** --- so a limit reaches **inward** freely and never **outward** | **Established, after a correction on 2026-08-09.** Three independent arrivals support the no-outward half (C18's hungry absence, C19's mis-scoped genre rule, the fault section). ~~**Under direct attack in the other direction** --- a tavern authored *poor* must reach inward, and this clause forbids it; the clause is required to both not-reach and reach, C11's internal-contradiction mode.~~ **Withdrawn: the contradiction was an artifact of the original wording**, which said *no further* without saying **in which direction**. A place setting's limit reaching the tavern is **outside** its holder-scope and forbidden; the tavern's limit reaching a place setting **inside** it is **within** its holder-scope and permitted. **Containment scope is directional by nature**, and reading *no further* as direction-neutral was the error. **Note what the clause does and does not do now:** it says a container's limits *may* reach inward; **which** of them do is [PL-1](#open-questions-p5-sub-questions-ids-stable-never-reused)'s question, not this clause's. The clause permits; relevance selects |
| **4** | **Licence is a first-class graph item, attached to an entity and manipulable** | **Open --- this is AB-42.** C19 shows a licence that *changes*, which supports *manipulable* but says nothing about node-versus-collection |
| **5** | **Licence import is a whole acquiring an intensional description** --- no mechanism of its own | **Established, on one case.** [C19](AGENT.abstractionLayers.corpus.planning.md#c19-filling-out-the-place-setting-and-the-tavern-that-ran-out-of-napkins) beat 3. Notable for falling out of C18's existing discriminator rather than needing new machinery, which is this plan's weak evidence-of-a-real-joint signal --- but one case is one case |
| **6** | **Import is a copy, not a reference; licence provenance is not traced and licence changes do not cascade** | **Settled as *policy*, with a re-open trigger --- a different category from the shape clauses and it must not be swept in with them.** See [Licence transfer](#licence-transfer-import-is-a-copy-and-provenance-is-deliberately-not-traced-2026-08-09). Defended on cost *and* on the local answer being the fictionally correct one |
| **7** | **Whether a whole has an *intensional* description or is merely *extensional* determines whether it has a licence at all** | **Established by C18 --- and corrected here.** C18 states the discriminator as *intensional -> open, extensional -> closed*. **That conflates two things.** A *poor* tavern is intensional and **narrow**, so intensionality determines whether a licence **exists and what shape it has**, not whether it is open. **Found by numbering, before C20 was written** |
| **8** | **Causality propagates; licence does not.** Permission is held, not transmitted --- register propagation policy *none* | **Established on one case (C19), and a deliberate asymmetry with [P4](#proposal-p4-settling-dataflow-and-narrative-heat) rather than an oversight.** Note P5 clause 3's attack does not touch this: *reaching inward by scope* is not the same as *propagating along edges* |
| **9** | **A licence is a *shaped space*, not a scalar** --- an entity may be open in some dimensions and closed in others | **Open, and unstated in P5 until now.** Surfaced by C20's *warm but poor* tavern: warmth and poverty are simultaneous and pull opposite ways, so a single openness dial cannot express it. **Puts real weight on AB-43's unsolved half** (describing a closed region) and on AB-42 (what a licence record is) |
| **10** | ~~**Entities not related by import have no licence relationship.**~~ **Restated 2026-08-09: permission is *copied* at import; context is *read live* via relevance** | ~~Open, the gap C20 targets second.~~ **Substantially resolved rather than falsified, and the resolution is the useful kind --- it names a distinction that was missing rather than removing one.** The original claim was a consequence of P5 clause 3 read direction-neutrally; once P5 clause 3 permits inward reach, a never-imported entity **does** have a relationship to its container --- it reads ancestor facts live, selected by [PL-1](#open-questions-p5-sub-questions-ids-stable-never-reused). **So *imported-from* and *merely-inside* differ as snapshot versus live read**, which also stops P5 clause 6's no-cascade from conflicting with live relevance: **the thing that does not cascade is *permission*; the thing read live is *context*.** Residual work belongs to PL-1, not here |
| **11** | **Authoring sets licence unattenuated; improvisation operates only within licence** | **Open --- proposed in discussion, never worked.** Authoring is not delegation, so it is outside any attenuation scheme; this is also the third route by which the asset-added branch of AB-29 arrives. C20 has an *authorially* constrained tavern, so it exercises this clause directly |

**Tagging convention, on H3's terms:** mark a row `P5-dependent` only when it is **derived from** a clause, not merely compatible with one --- and **cite the clause number**, since the whole point of numbering is that P5 clause 6 falling should not revert rows that only ever needed P5 clause 2.

### What the reframe buys, stated as four specific repairs

| Problem, as previously recorded | Under P5 |
| --- | --- |
| **The completeness trap** ([C18](AGENT.abstractionLayers.corpus.planning.md#c18-three-place-settings-and-only-two-of-them-may-have-a-napkin) Finding 4) --- *no napkin here* must not be read as *no napkins in the tavern* | **Becomes unrepresentable rather than forbidden.** The limit attaches to the place setting, so it cannot reach the tavern. **A rule became an invariant**, which is the stronger form and the reason to prefer this framing over the warning it replaces |
| **AB-43** --- what is a negative keyed by, when its referent does not exist? | **Dissolved for the blanket case** (keyed by the licensing entity, an id that exists) and **reshaped for the narrowing case**: no longer *give identity to a nonentity*, which is ill-posed, but *describe a closed region of licence space*, which is well-posed and comparably hard |
| **AB-42's accumulation cost** --- negatives are cheaper to create than positives and accrue faster | **Collapses from N nodes to one licence per entity whose content narrows.** And a licence **coarsens** (*this place setting is closed*) where independent negative nodes do not --- which matters precisely because the cost was about LLM context |
| **AB-45's provenance collision** --- licence is provenance-determined, but AB-14 holds provenance is invisible downstream | **Resolved.** Licence is carried *by the whole* as a first-class property; provenance only sets its initial value. Nothing downstream introspects how the whole came to exist, so the merge-layer claim survives intact |

**One consistency check, run deliberately because a unification that quietly costs a settled property is the failure mode:** *not established* still costs nothing. Licence is recorded only when **narrowed**, so C17's three-value model is preserved rather than traded away.

### What it does not resolve

- **AB-43 for narrowing licences** --- describing a closed region is still unanswered.
- **Whether licence-closure and established-absence are the same fact.** *There is no battery* must be a **world** fact for the deduction *therefore it is dark* to run; *this place setting is closed* is only a permission. They plausibly coincide, since the only route to a battery appearing there is improvisation --- **except through authoring**, which is not delegated and can add a deep fryer to a kitchen whose licence is closed. **That exception is [AB-29](AGENT.abstractionLayers.planning.md#open-decisions-design--plan-only)'s asset-added branch arriving from a third direction.** AB-44 therefore survives in narrower form: **every established negative closes licence; only some also carry causal force.**
- **Composition of licences** --- how nested and layered licences combine, and how positives and negatives merge under manipulation. **Open; the leading frame is capability attenuation** (a delegated authority may only narrow), with **authoring explicitly outside it**. **Subsumed as the licence-shaped half of [PL-1](#open-questions-p5-sub-questions-ids-stable-never-reused)**, which asks the same question over positive facts as well.
- **Divergence** --- see the cost below.

### Licence transfer: import is a copy, and provenance is deliberately not traced (2026-08-09)

**The operation, from [C19](AGENT.abstractionLayers.corpus.planning.md#c19-filling-out-the-place-setting-and-the-tavern-that-ran-out-of-napkins).** A recognized place setting is licence-zero. The player says *"fill out the place setting with everything else it needs."* Licence is **imported** from the tavern --- which has an open licence to improvise tavern furnishings --- into the place setting, whose limit is removed. **It thereafter holds its own, independent licence.**

**What the import actually is, and it is not a new mechanism.** [C18](AGENT.abstractionLayers.corpus.planning.md#c18-three-place-settings-and-only-two-of-them-may-have-a-napkin)'s discriminator is **intensional versus extensional**: a recognized whole is extensional --- its member set *is* its content --- which is exactly why its licence is zero. The player's utterance supplies an **intensional description** (*everything a place setting needs*). **Licence import is a whole acquiring an intensional description.** **So C18's three cases are three *states*, not three kinds, and there is an operation between them** --- which C18 did not say and, read alone, implies the opposite. **The negative lock is not permanent**, and nothing in the model made it so.

**Import is a copy, not a reference.** Once imported, the licence is the place setting's own. A later tavern-level narrowing --- *the tavern has no napkins in stock* --- **does not reach into it**, and the place setting may still improvise a napkin.

**Explicitly declined, and recorded as a decision rather than an omission: licence provenance is not traced and licence changes do not cascade.** It is *possible* to keep the provenance graph of every imported licence and propagate narrowings through it. **It is not worth it:** an immense standing cost --- every licence read potentially walking a history, every narrowing potentially cascading --- to correct a small set of edge cases. **This plan is choosing not to be constrained by it.**

**And the local reading is usually the *fictionally correct* one, which upgrades this from a compromise to a preference.** The napkin was laid out before anyone found the storeroom empty; a tavern being out of napkins **now** is a claim about stock, not about what is already set on a table. **The cheaper answer is also the better one in the motivating case**, which is worth stating so this is not remembered as a scalability concession.

**The one case where it does bite, and the repair is scope rather than propagation.** If *no napkins* is meant as a **world or genre rule** rather than a stock fact, a place setting improvising one is genuinely wrong. **The fix is the move that killed the scope trap, arriving a third time:** attach the limit at the scope it actually holds at. A genre rule is not the tavern's licence and should never have been recorded as one. **Propagation is the wrong repair for a mis-scoped limit.**

**The principle this establishes, stated because it is a deliberate asymmetry with [P4](#proposal-p4-settling-dataflow-and-narrative-heat):** **causality propagates; licence does not.** Permission has no conservation law and no downstream obligation --- a licence is held, not transmitted. As a [relation-kind register](AGENT.abstractionLayers.planning.md#the-relation-kind-register) entry, licence's propagation policy is **none**, which no other kind in the register has.

**Re-open trigger, set now while it costs nothing --- and set at *consequence*, not at visibility.** ~~A player-visible contradiction.~~ **Raised 2026-08-09, the same day, and the correction matters:** player-visible is the **minimum** bar, not the bar. Per [Fiction is a fault-tolerant medium](AGENT.abstractionLayers.planning.md#fiction-is-a-fault-tolerant-medium--and-what-that-does-not-excuse-2026-08-09), a contradiction a player merely *notices* is inside the tolerance --- audiences do repair work, and the alternatives to accepting that are all worse. **The trigger is a contradiction a player *acted on*** --- reasoning from an absence and being contradicted by the world is agency undermined --- **or one that breaks a stake the fiction had established.** Also unconditionally: **any *structural* defect** (two napkins where there is one, a mis-keyed referent) is a bug regardless, never covered by this decision, and never a reason to re-open it either. **And weight the trigger by salience** --- a contradiction the fiction never leaned on is cheap even when noticed; one that contradicts something a scene was *about* is not.

### Locked: P5's shape clauses (2026-08-09)

**On [the parts-and-ports precedent](AGENT.abstractionLayers.planning.md#locked-frame-parts-and-ports-2026-08-06), and with the same discipline: lock the shape, enumerate loudly what must not ride in on it.**

**Locked --- P5 clauses 1, 2, 3 and 7.** Unbounded by default; a negative is a recorded **narrowing** (of two kinds, **content** and **manner**); scope is the entity **plus what it contains**, so limits reach inward and never outward; and **intensional versus extensional** decides whether a whole has a licence and what shape it takes. **Each is corpus-grounded rather than argued** --- P5 clause 1 was attacked by [C20](AGENT.abstractionLayers.corpus.planning.md#c20-the-locked-room-and-nothing-obvious) and vindicated, P5 clause 2 was falsified by C20 and **locks in its repaired form**, P5 clause 3 has three independent arrivals, P5 clause 7 comes from [C18](AGENT.abstractionLayers.corpus.planning.md#c18-three-place-settings-and-only-two-of-them-may-have-a-napkin) and was corrected by the numbering pass.

**Not locked, and must not ride in on this.** **Representation** (P5 clause 4 / AB-42 --- node or collection, undecided). **Composition** of nested licences (PL-1's licence-shaped half, unworked; capability attenuation still an unexercised candidate). **Narrative weight** (AB-46, deliberately undesigned). **Transfer-is-copy** (P5 clause 6) --- settled as *policy with a re-open trigger*, **a different category from a shape claim**, and it must not acquire lock status by proximity. **Licence as the generative half of setting character** (speculative). **Genre boundaries** (AB-47) --- **a different construct entirely**; locking P5 locks nothing about them.

**Consequences accepted by locking, recorded so they are owned rather than rediscovered.** **(i)** Manner narrowings require **judgment to apply**, so AB-43 has no purely descriptive answer for some regions --- locked with that cost attached rather than pending its resolution. **(ii)** P5's **utility** remains gated on [PL-1 iteration 1](#pl-1-iteration-1-brute-force-relevance-classified-as-a-prototype-2026-08-09); only its **shape** is locked. **(iii)** **Locking ahead of a killing test is deliberate.** No case has yet tried to destroy P5 --- C20's worst outcome was a repair --- and the mitigation is the trigger below rather than a claim that none exists.

**What would re-open a locked clause --- the proposal-level rollback trigger, set now.** **If licence records turn out to be reconstructible from ordinary established facts, with no behaviour distinguishing them, then licence is not a construct and P5 failed** --- negatives-as-narrowings would collapse back into plain facts wearing a new name. **Measurable from iteration 1's data, and it fires against the proposal rather than the implementation**, which is the property [P4's own trigger](#what-p4-being-load-bearing-here-means-2026-08-07) was built to have. **Second re-opener:** a case exhibiting a **true internal contradiction** --- one restriction that must be simultaneously in force and not in force for a single entity, [C11](AGENT.abstractionLayers.corpus.planning.md#c11-the-rube-goldberg-machine)'s mode. **Named so the record shows this was considered and not merely unimagined; no candidate scenario exists.**

#### Why the paradox re-opener is less threatening here than in a simulationist model (2026-08-09)

**The concern is real for the wrong kind of system.** In a deductive knowledge representation a contradiction is catastrophic because of **explosion** --- from a contradiction, anything follows, so one bad fact corrupts every inference downstream of it. **That is the reason logical KR treats paradox as fatal**, and it is worth naming, because inheriting the fear without inheriting the mechanism would be a mistake.

**This system does no deductive closure.** It assembles a bounded packet and hands it to a reasoning step. **A contradiction therefore does not propagate --- it sits there being odd**, and the blast radius is bounded by the medium rather than by the logic. **Under [the feedback-regime frame](AGENT.abstractionLayers.planning.md#the-feedback-regime-is-the-thing-to-measure-and-it-decides-which-solutions-to-look-for-2026-08-09) a contradiction is maximally *amplifying* in a deductive system and *damping* in a narration system** --- nobody builds on it, and narration papers it over. **Third time that frame has predicted a result before the argument for it was made.**

**And the medium is on our side in a way a simulation's is not: fiction is *supposed* to make sense.** Simulated reality routinely produces edge cases nobody intended, because it is not authored toward coherence; **fiction is produced by minds that want it to hang together, and audiences actively repair what does not.** Where fiction does contain paradox it is nearly always **owned as content** --- time travel, unreliable narration --- rather than suffered as a system failure.

**The adversarial case is handled by machinery already adopted, not by consistency maintenance.** A player *constructing* a paradox on purpose is attempting a mint; the [gate refuses and the system steadily refuses to reason about it](#the-cost-that-is-not-optional-and-it-is-ab-19s). **We never have to resolve the paradox --- only decline to build on it** --- and the refusal is already a moderation signal per [C21 Finding 7](AGENT.abstractionLayers.corpus.planning.md#finding-7-the-gate-produces-moderation-instrumentation-for-free-and-that-may-be-the-larger-prize).

#### How fiction actually resolves a contradiction: it registers a mystery (2026-08-09)

**The stronger form of the argument above, and it names a mechanism rather than a disposition.** *Elf King Ozymand died twenty thousand years ago* and *Elf King Ozymand fought the great drake Grimhond two hundred years ago* are flatly contradictory. **A play-to-find-out GM does not reconcile them and does not retract either.** They say *"Right? Sounds impossible. I wonder what's going on there,"* and move on --- **the contradiction registered as a mystery, to be solved through play or ignored.**

**Three things follow, and the first is the one that matters most.**

1. **No retraction is required, and that is the fourth arrival of this plan's *not a knowledge base* theme.** A logic KR must retract one fact or quarantine both. **Fiction keeps both**, because the graph holds **established fiction**, not asserted truths --- the same move [P4's dataflow reframe](#retraction-dissolves-under-a-dataflow-reading-and-that-reading-is-the-ludic-native-one) made when it found *the graph holds states, not beliefs* and retraction dissolved. **Two independent routes to *do not retract*, from causality and from consistency.**
2. **This is [C20](AGENT.abstractionLayers.corpus.planning.md#c20-the-locked-room-and-nothing-obvious)'s mystery construct, generalised --- and C20 found it first for a different input.** *Nothing obvious* converts an **unknown** into a mystery; Ozymand converts a **contradiction** into one. **Same downstream object** --- a flagged question carrying a promise that an answer exists and is worth earning --- **from opposite inputs** (too little established, versus too much and conflicting). **So *mystery* is not a puzzle-specific device; it is the system's general defer-with-interest primitive**, and it needs no new state: a mystery is a **decorated not-established**, inheriting [C17](AGENT.abstractionLayers.corpus.planning.md#c17-the-professional-kitchen-the-chefs-knife-and-the-stand-mixer-nobody-asked-about)'s *settled, not pending* property.
3. **"Or ignored" is load-bearing and easy to skim past.** A registered mystery carries **no obligation** to be resolved. That is what makes it cost one sentence, and it is why this does not smuggle in the unbounded-worklist problem [PS-10](#open-questions-p4-sub-questions-ids-stable-never-reused) is still wrestling with on the impulse side.

**Prior art, named on the same footing as reincorporation.** *Play to find out what happens* is Apocalypse World's agenda, and this is the **second load-bearing frame this plan has taken from theatre and tabletop craft** rather than from computing --- after [Johnstone's reincorporation](AGENT.abstractionLayers.planning.md#narrative-integrity-is-in-service-to-reincorporation-2026-08-09). Worth noticing as a pattern in where the useful answers have been coming from.

**The precondition the mechanism has, and it is not optional: mystery is cheap only where nobody is vested.** Registering *I wonder what's going on there* defers reconciliation --- **and deferral is free exactly when no one is waiting on the answer.** If a player were invested in Ozymand having been continuously dead for twenty thousand years, contradicting it risks precisely the injury [narrative integrity](AGENT.abstractionLayers.planning.md#narrative-integrity-is-in-service-to-reincorporation-2026-08-09) exists to prevent: their contribution not being valued or built on. **Deferring then is not neutral --- it leaves them waiting to learn whether their contribution survived**, which is an unresolved social tension rather than an intriguing loose end.

**That is why GM fiction contradicting *itself* is so much easier than players contradicting *each other*, and the provenance split already predicts the whole gradient:**

| Contradiction between | Cost |
| --- | --- |
| **system-improvised ↔ system-improvised** | **Trivial.** Mystery, or simply pick one. Nobody is vested |
| **system-improvised ↔ authored or player-driven** | **Asymmetric and easy.** The weighted side stands; the improvised side yields |
| **authored ↔ player-driven** | Hard --- and it is [AB-29](AGENT.abstractionLayers.planning.md#open-decisions-design--plan-only)'s asset-added branch, already open |
| **player-driven ↔ player-driven** | **Hardest, and the mystery move does not defuse it** --- both contributors are waiting, so deferral costs rather than saves |

**Two consequences worth carrying.** **(i) [AB-46](AGENT.abstractionLayers.planning.md#open-decisions-design--plan-only) gains a third job: weight decides whether a contradiction may be *deferred*.** After *how much contradicting it strains* and *the effort required to overturn it*, that is **three independent uses landing on one scalar**, which is evidence the quantity is real rather than an annotation invented per problem. **(ii) The favourable half, and it bears directly on [the feedback regime](AGENT.abstractionLayers.planning.md#the-feedback-regime-is-the-thing-to-measure-and-it-decides-which-solutions-to-look-for-2026-08-09):** in this system the GM **is** the LLM, so **machine-generated fiction is system-improvised and therefore the *lowest-weight* kind by construction.** The contradictions an LLM in the loop produces are overwhelmingly the absorbable ones; **the expensive contradictions are player-against-player, which the LLM is not the author of.** That is a real narrowing of the fault-propagation worry rather than a reassurance about it.

**And where deferral is not affordable, the repair is not to pick a winner.** The craft move is to find the reading in which **both** contributions stand --- which is what the Ozymand mystery does when nobody is vested, and what must actually be *performed* rather than promised when someone is. The out-of-character channel [AB-47](AGENT.abstractionLayers.planning.md#open-decisions-design--plan-only) identifies for table negotiation is the fallback when even that fails.

##### Correction: a mystery caps a *minimum effort*, not a maximum time (2026-08-09)

~~***Mystery* is the degenerate, zero-stakes case of reconciliation, not a separate mechanism.**~~ **Withdrawn the same day, and it had the construct backwards --- it described a floor as a ceiling.** That framing reads a mystery as **deferral**: reconciliation postponed, resolution arriving eventually. **The actual point of a first-class mystery object is the opposite: it caps a *minimum effort*** --- *this is unknown and **will not be revealed** before a certain amount of player investment.* **The floor is the whole construct.** A mystery whose answer is available on request is worthless; the locked room would be solved by asking.

**Three things this fixes, and the first dissolves a separate construct rather than adding one.**

1. **A mystery *is* a [manner narrowing](#the-scheme-as-a-clause-table-numbered-2026-08-09), applied to something not yet established.** [C20](AGENT.abstractionLayers.corpus.planning.md#c20-the-locked-room-and-nothing-obvious) split narrowings into **content** (a set that may not be minted) and **manner** (a way of minting that is not permitted). **A mystery is exactly a manner narrowing on an unknown: the answer may be established, but only by an earned manner.** So this needs no new construct --- it is P5 clause 2's repair, reaching the case that motivated it.
2. **It *creates* stakes rather than lacking them, which is what the withdrawn phrasing got wrong.** Registering a mystery says *this is worth something and it will cost you*. Calling that zero-stakes inverted the claim.
3. **"Or ignored" stops needing separate justification.** Under a deferral reading, an unresolved mystery is a dangling loose end requiring an argument for why that is acceptable. **Under a floor reading it is simply *nobody paid the price*** --- a complete and correct outcome, not an omission.

**And it makes the construct mechanically real, which *first-class mystery object* was already asserting.** A minimum-investment floor is a **predicate a gate can evaluate** --- has the work been done? --- where *deferred reconciliation* is not checkable at all.

**One extension to [AB-46](AGENT.abstractionLayers.planning.md#open-decisions-design--plan-only) follows, and it is symmetric enough to be worth having.** That row defines weight as **effort required to overturn**, which applies to things already established. **A mystery has no content and still has a floor** --- so the same scalar runs the other way as **effort required to *establish***. **One quantity, measured forward for the not-yet-established and backward for the established**, covering the whole lifecycle rather than half of it.

**The limit, stated so this does not get over-applied --- and it is the well-formedness line again.** **Contradictory *fiction* absorbs; contradictory *machinery* does not.** A mint gate must return yes or no, so a licence that both permits and forbids is not a mystery to be registered --- it is a code path that cannot decide, and it needs a **tie-break rule** (fail-safe: refuse). **That reduces the second re-opener further without dissolving it:** the machinery needs a defensible tie-break, and a case where no sensible tie-break exists would still be a real problem.

**What none of this excuses, on the standing discipline:** a paradox that produces a **structural** defect --- two place settings where there was one --- is still a bug. **Coherence is negotiable; well-formedness is not**, and that line does not move because the contradiction was interesting.

### Prior art in shipped code: Coyote's two affordance branches (2026-08-09)

**Checked against code, not recalled.** [`coyotePlanAffinities.ts`](../../../../../packages/mtw-interfaces/ts/coyotePlanAffinities.ts) gives `CoyoteTropeAffinity` two optional affordance fields, and **they are not two flavours of one thing --- they are the container side and the contributor side, and they differ on every axis that matters here.**

| | `environmentAffordances` | `affordancesProvided` |
| --- | --- | --- |
| **Shape** | `{ object: EnvironmentAffordanceObject; roles }` | `{ object: string; intended?: true; roles }` |
| **Vocabulary** | **Closed enum** --- `boulder \| cactus \| tumbleweed \| rock-wall \| long-fall` | **Open string** |
| **Who supplies it** | The **location** | The **item** |
| **Container filtering** | [`filterTropeAffinitiesByRoom.ts`](../../../../../lambda/ephemera/dataSource/objects/filterTropeAffinitiesByRoom.ts) **removes** entries not available in the room | **None**, and the module says so explicitly: *"Room rules filter only `environmentAffordances`"* |
| **Direction** | **Constrains** --- the room narrows what a staged object may claim | **Contributes** --- the item adds to what is available |

**Five things this maps onto --- and one seam to declare first, because it bounds all of them.** **Coyote is a *reasoning-axis* system and P5 is an *authoring-axis* proposal.** `filterTropeAffinitiesByRoom` governs what a reasoning step is **shown**; P5's licence governs what may be **minted**. The mapping below is therefore strongest for [PL-1](#open-questions-p5-sub-questions-ids-stable-never-reused) --- which *is* the reasoning-axis question inside P5 --- and weakest for the licence clauses. **Recorded because the first draft of this section did not flag the seam and over-claimed on the strength of it.**

1. **[P5 clause 3](#the-scheme-as-a-clause-table-numbered-2026-08-09)'s inward reach has a shipped *analogue*.** `filterTropeAffinitiesByRoom(roomId)(tropeAffinities)` takes a **room** and narrows what an **object staged in it** may claim --- a container reaching a contained entity. **Downgraded from *instance* to *analogue* 2026-08-09, same day, on the correction below:** what the room narrows is the object's **reasoning inputs**, not its licence, so this confirms the *direction* is natural and workable rather than confirming the clause itself. **Still worth having** --- the shape this plan spent a day mistakenly calling a contradiction is running in production --- but it is not the clause.
2. **[PL-1](#open-questions-p5-sub-questions-ids-stable-never-reused) is shipped in hardcoded form.** That filter *is* relevance selection: of the environment facts a container holds, which reach the contained thing's reasoning? Its answer is a `switch` over three hardcoded room sets (`ROOM_IDS_WITH_ROCK_WALL`, `ROOM_IDS_WITH_LONG_FALL`, `ROOM_IDS_WITHOUT_CACTUS`). **Seventh time this initiative has found infrastructure pre-registered for a need it had not yet articulated** --- and the first time the pre-registration is of the *central open question* rather than a mechanism.
3. **Enforcement happens at *context-assembly* time --- a third enforcement point, and probably the best one.** [AB-47](AGENT.abstractionLayers.planning.md#open-decisions-design--plan-only) settled on a **mint-time** gate and rejected a **narration-time** one. Coyote does neither: it filters what the reasoning step is *shown*. **If `rock-wall` is not in the packet, no plan proposes one and there is nothing to refuse.** That is the *refusal is structurally silent* insight, already implemented --- prevention by **omission** rather than by rejection, and it does not need a refusal message because nothing was attempted.
4. **`aptness` is a shipped relevance threshold.** `COYOTE_AFFINITY_APTNESS_MIN = 0.2`, with entries below it dropped. Graded relevance with a cutoff, which is PL-1's shape again.
5. **`intended?: true` is a candidate weight marker.** It distinguishes affordances an object was *meant* to provide from incidental ones --- plausibly a two-valued **AB-46**. **Flagged, not asserted**: its consumers were not traced, and a boolean is a thin basis for a claim about a graded concept.

#### A closed *reasoning vocabulary*, not a closed *world* --- and the difference is the whole question (corrected 2026-08-09)

~~**The falsification: P5 clause 1 is wrong for closed vocabularies, and shipped code says so.** Environment affordances are closed-world: a room absent from `ROOM_IDS_WITH_ROCK_WALL` has no rock wall, silence means **no**, and that is exactly what P5 clause 1 denies.~~ **Over-claimed, and withdrawn the same day it was written.** `filterTropeAffinitiesByRoom` says *do not reason with a rock wall here*; it does **not** say *there is no rock wall here*. **Those coincide in Coyote only because nothing else ever surfaces environment objects** --- which is exactly the *circumstantial, not structural* criticism [Coyote as prior art](AGENT.abstractionLayers.planning.md#coyote-as-prior-art-bounded-llm-reasoning-that-is-not-bounded-structurally) already makes, arriving here against a claim this plan had just made itself.

**What survives is narrower and still useful: a closed *reasoning* vocabulary is legitimate, shipped, and correct** --- because the five entries are authored and exhaustive **for the purpose of bounding a reasoning packet.**

**The enum's selection criterion, checked rather than assumed, because it sharpens this:** the five are not desert nouns. `long-fall` is not an object anyone finds --- it is a property of a place. Boulder, cactus, tumbleweed, rock-wall and long-fall are the **standard Coyote gag affordances**; **lizards and sand are perfectly desert-justified and excluded anyway**, because neither affords a gag. **The vocabulary was drawn around what the reasoning step needs, not around what the setting contains** --- which is why reading it as a claim about the world was wrong.

**And the open side proves the point:** `affordancesProvided` is an open string, so `{ object: 'lizard', roles: ['Bait'] }` is representable today. **The closed enum constrains the *environment as a source*, not the fiction.** A thing may provide a lizard; the desert may not.

**The rule that survives, stated for the reasoning axis where it belongs:** *things a professional kitchen might have* is unbounded, so no packet can enumerate it and [C17](AGENT.abstractionLayers.corpus.planning.md#c17-the-professional-kitchen-the-chefs-knife-and-the-stand-mixer-nobody-asked-about)'s three values are forced. *Environment affordances* is **five items**, authored and exhaustive, so a packet can carry the whole vocabulary and **absence from it is decidable**. **The three-value model collapses to two whenever the vocabulary is genuinely enumerable --- for the consumer that only sees the packet.** Whether it also collapses for the **world** is C20's question, not this section's.

**So [C20](AGENT.abstractionLayers.planning.md#recommended-order) is *not* pre-answered, and P5 clause 1 is not falsified.** The question it owns is intact and now stated more precisely: **does a closed *reasoning* vocabulary license a closed *world*?** Coyote never faces it, because in a five-room demo the two are indistinguishable. **What C20 gains is a worked example of the legitimate half** --- closed vocabularies bounding a packet --- **and a sharpened axis to isolate: vocabulary-openness, and whether an author may close an open domain by fiat.**

**One general principle falls out of the vocabulary asymmetry, and it explains why [AB-43](AGENT.abstractionLayers.planning.md#open-decisions-design--plan-only) is hard.** The **constraining** side is a closed enum; the **contributing** side is an open string. That is not incidental --- **a constraint needs a decidable membership test** (does this proposed thing fall in the closed region?) **and a contribution does not** (it just adds). **Minting is easy and narrowing is hard for that reason**, which is AB-43's difficulty restated as a property rather than a puzzle.

### Open questions (P5 sub-questions; ids stable, never reused)

**Parallel to P3's PQ series and P4's PS series.**

| ID | Question |
| --- | --- |
| **PL-1** | **Of all the positive and negative facts held by containing abstractions higher in the fractal, how do we reasonably determine which are *relevant* to improvisation at a local level?** **The central open question of this proposal**, and the one [C20](AGENT.abstractionLayers.planning.md#recommended-order) exists to pressure. **Note three things about the framing, each load-bearing.** **(i) It spans positive facts as well as negative ones**, so it is wider than licence: a tavern authored with a roaring fire should inform local improvisation (soot, shadows, warmth) without that fact being a permission at all. **P5's licence frame covers only the negative channel**, and this question is the reason not to mistake the proposal for the whole mechanism. **(ii) *Relevant*, deliberately, and not *salient*** --- [salience is already spoken for](AGENT.abstractionLayers.planning.md#narrative-integrity-is-in-service-to-reincorporation-2026-08-09), where it measures how much contradicting a fact would strain, and the two come apart (a player's *my character hates cats* is strain-expensive and irrelevant to a place setting; the tavern's napkin stock is the reverse). **Relevance is also the word [PS-4](#open-questions-p4-sub-questions-ids-stable-never-reused) already uses for context selection, which is correct rather than coincidental --- see (iii).** **(iii) This is the initiative's founding question arriving inside P5.** *Which ancestor facts belong in this packet* is [C3](AGENT.abstractionLayers.corpus.planning.md#c3-the-snare-trap-coyote-cannot-name)'s scoping problem, AB-22's affinity filter and AB-24's ambient tier, one level in. **Reassuring** --- no new hard problem is being invented --- **and cautionary**, since P5 thereby inherits an unsolved one. **Iteration 1 elected 2026-08-09: candidate 6 (LLM judgment at mint time), in its most naive brute-force form, classified as a Prototype --- see [PL-1 iteration 1](#pl-1-iteration-1-brute-force-relevance-classified-as-a-prototype-2026-08-09) for the fit, the two cautions, and the rollback triggers.** **Electing one does not close the row:** iteration 2 is expected to be *annotate at mint time* (candidate: precomputed per ancestor-fact/entity-kind), and the remaining candidates stay live as refinements or replacements. **Candidate answers, listed to keep the space open rather than to rank them:** copy-at-import ([C19](AGENT.abstractionLayers.corpus.planning.md#c19-filling-out-the-place-setting-and-the-tavern-that-ran-out-of-napkins)'s shipped-in-design mechanism, and **partial by construction** --- it says nothing about entities that never imported, which is P5 clause 10's gap); kind-based (some fact kinds containment-transitive, others local); weight-based (**AB-46**); depth- or distance-based; semantic proximity (the objects lane already carries embeddings); LLM judgment at mint time; explicit authorial declaration of reach. **The illustration to reason from, inherited 2026-08-09 from the retired C20 and sharper here than it was there.** A tavern authored **poor** containing a place setting: poverty should plausibly reach inward and stop silver flatware being improvised. A tavern **out of napkins**: [C19](AGENT.abstractionLayers.corpus.planning.md#c19-filling-out-the-place-setting-and-the-tavern-that-ran-out-of-napkins) beat 5 says it need **not** reach the same place setting. **Structurally identical, and the discriminator is therefore not structural.** **What settles it is a causal story, not a rule:** *perhaps the tavern is out of napkins precisely because the last one was laid on that place setting* --- under which the napkin exists and explains the shortage. **And the opposite answer is equally fine:** judge no-napkins relevant and the setting simply lacks one. **Both produce coherent fiction, which is the strongest evidence that relevance is *reasoning* rather than retrieval** --- and therefore that the answer to this row is an [AB-19](AGENT.abstractionLayers.planning.md#open-decisions-design--plan-only)-style bounded LLM step over a well-chosen packet, not a lookup table. **Expecting a rule to adjudicate poverty-versus-napkins was the wrong shape of expectation**, and it cost a falsification case to notice. **[Back-swept 2026-08-10 (tranche C)](#the-parts-and-ports-back-sweep-2026-08-10): the sixth member of [the clause-2 direction cluster](#finding-6-the-clause-2-direction-cluster-reaches-seven-and-one-member-sequences-work-on-the-answer), and the first that reads **outward**.** *Which facts held by containing abstractions higher in the fractal are relevant locally* is untouched by locked-frame clause 2's gloss and forbidden by its headline --- and if the headline is the clause, *copy-at-import* stops being one candidate among seven and becomes the only legal family. **This row is not parked**: iteration 1 is an elected Prototype whose naive form assembles a packet of ancestor facts, which is the built thing tranche A hoped to get ahead of. 

**One distinction this row now owns, promoted from P5 clause 10:** **permission is *copied* at import; context is *read live*.** That is what keeps P5 clause 6's no-cascade from conflicting with live relevance, and it is the difference between *imported-from* and *merely-inside*.

**Shipped prior art, found 2026-08-09 and it is this row's most concrete input:** [`filterTropeAffinitiesByRoom.ts`](../../../../../lambda/ephemera/dataSource/objects/filterTropeAffinitiesByRoom.ts) **is this question already answered, badly and on purpose** --- a container (room) narrowing which of its facts reach a contained object's reasoning, resolved by a `switch` over three hardcoded room sets. **Its answer does not generalise** (that is the *circumstantial, not structural* criticism [Coyote as prior art](AGENT.abstractionLayers.planning.md#coyote-as-prior-art-bounded-llm-reasoning-that-is-not-bounded-structurally) already makes), **but its shape is exactly this row's**, and it enforces at **context-assembly** time rather than at mint time --- filter what the reasoning step is shown, and there is nothing to refuse. **Any candidate below should be checked against whether it could replace that switch.**

**One check already available against the kind-based candidate:** a fixed table of which fact kinds propagate is close to the compiled-vocabulary move **AB-19 rejected**, so it needs a fallback rather than an enumeration --- which is a real constraint on it, not a disqualification |

### PL-1 iteration 1: brute-force relevance, classified as a Prototype (2026-08-09)

**A candidate is elected rather than a new one proposed.** [PL-1](#open-questions-p5-sub-questions-ids-stable-never-reused) lists seven; **iteration 1 is candidate 6, *LLM judgment at mint time*, in its most naive form**: hand a reasoning step **every** permission restriction held above the current node in the fractal, together with the candidate improvisation, and let it judge which bear. **It works and it does not scale**, which is precisely [P4](#what-p4-being-load-bearing-here-means-2026-08-07)'s shape.

**Classified as a *Prototype*, on P4's precedent and for P4's reason.** Not a Proposal (which costs only design time to roll back) and not a Working Hypothesis. **Code will exist before iterations 2+ are designed, so a rollback means deciding what to do with shipped code that was known going in to be non-scalable** --- sunk *engineering* cost, which pulls harder than sunk reasoning ever did. **Tag dependent rows `PL-1-i1-dependent`**, and cite what they actually need, per the over-tagging warning [the tagging test](#the-tagging-test-and-why-it-is-the-point) already gives.
